      <p class="text-slate-300 mb-1">Payment received — thank you!</p>
      <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-6">You’ll receive a confirmation email shortly with setup instructions for your new business email address.</p>
      <a href="https://registermysite.com/" class="btn-primary inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl">
        Back to RegisterMySite
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ============================================================
    //  CONFIGURATION — edit these values
    // ============================================================
    const FORM_EMAIL = 'business-email-signup@registermysite.com';   // ← email that receives signup notifications

    const STRIPE = {
      /**
       * OPTION A (recommended for static sites — zero backend)
       * Create 3 Payment Links in Stripe Dashboard (Products → Payment Links)
       * Set each to recurring monthly billing ($5, $12, $20).
       * Optional: set Success URL to this page + ?success=1
       *           and Cancel URL to this page + ?canceled=1
       */
      paymentLinks: {
        starter:      'https://buy.stripe.com/test_8x2cMY2mfd6zbGJ2Fd0gw00', // e.g. 'https://buy.stripe.com/test_xxxxxxxx'
        professional: 'https://buy.stripe.com/test_9B6bIU0mUbJr5o24d1co000', // e.g. 'https://buy.stripe.com/test_yyyyyyyy'
        complete:     'https://buy.stripe.com/test_7sY4gsaSL1nRfWZcfN0gw01'  // e.g. 'https://buy.stripe.com/test_zzzzzzzz'
      },

      /**
       * OPTION B (dynamic Checkout Sessions — requires a small backend)
       * Point this to an endpoint that accepts POST { plan, email, name, desired_email, ... }
       * and returns { url: "https://checkout.stripe.com/..." }
       * Leave empty to use Payment Links instead.
       */
      createSessionEndpoint: '' // e.g. '/api/create-checkout-session'
    };
    // ============================================================

    // ========== State ==========
    let selectedPlan = null;
    let formDataCache = null;

    // ========== DOM ==========
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#pricing-cards article');
    const form = document.getElementById('signup-form');
    const formPlan = document.getElementById('form-plan');
    const formPrice = document.getElementById('form-price');
    const planLabel = document.getElementById('selected-plan-label');
    const planDisplayName = document.getElementById('plan-display-name');
    const planDisplayPrice = document.getElementById('plan-display-price');
    const domainSuffix = document.getElementById('domain-suffix');
    const customDomainGroup = document.getElementById('custom-domain-group');
    const customDomainInput = document.getElementById('custom-domain');
    const localPartInput = document.getElementById('local-part');
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
    const formError = document.getElementById('form-error');
    const redirectModal = document.getElementById('redirect-modal');
    const configWarning = document.getElementById('config-warning');
    const successModal = document.getElementById('success-modal');
    const menuBtn = document.getElementById('menu-btn');
    const mobileMenu = document.getElementById('mobile-menu');

    // ========== Show success modal if returned from Stripe ==========
    (function checkReturnFromStripe() {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      if (params.get('success') === '1') {
        successModal.classList.add('open');
        successModal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        // Clean the URL so refresh doesn't re-show
        window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
      }
    })();

    // ========== Mobile menu ==========
    menuBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      mobileMenu.classList.toggle('hidden');
    });

    // ========== Plan selection ==========
    cards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
        selectPlan(card);
      });
      card.querySelector('.select-plan-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        selectPlan(card);
        document.getElementById('signup-form-section').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      });
    });

    function selectPlan(card) {
      cards.forEach(c => {
        c.classList.remove('card-selected');
        const btn = c.querySelector('.select-plan-btn');
        btn.classList.remove('btn-primary');
        btn.classList.add('btn-secondary');
        if (c.dataset.plan === 'professional') {
          // featured card keeps its visual weight
        }
      });
      card.classList.add('card-selected');
      const btn = card.querySelector('.select-plan-btn');
      btn.classList.remove('btn-secondary');
      btn.classList.add('btn-primary');
      btn.textContent = 'Selected ✓';

      selectedPlan = {
        id: card.dataset.plan,
        name: card.dataset.name,
        price: parseInt(card.dataset.price, 10)
      };

      formPlan.value = selectedPlan.id;
      formPrice.value = selectedPlan.price;
      planDisplayName.textContent = selectedPlan.name;
      planDisplayPrice.textContent = selectedPlan.price;
      planLabel.classList.remove('hidden');

      if (selectedPlan.id === 'starter') {
        domainSuffix.textContent = '@registermysite.com';
        customDomainGroup.classList.add('hidden');
        customDomainInput.removeAttribute('required');
        customDomainInput.value = '';
      } else {
        domainSuffix.textContent = '@yourdomain.com';
        customDomainGroup.classList.remove('hidden');
        customDomainInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
      }
    }

    // Pre-select Professional (most popular)
    document.querySelector('[data-plan="professional"]').click();

    function getDesiredEmail() {
      const local = (localPartInput.value || 'you').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (selectedPlan && selectedPlan.id === 'starter') {
        return `${local}@registermysite.com`;
      }
      const domain = (customDomainInput.value || 'yourdomain.com')
        .toLowerCase().trim()
        .replace(/^https?:\/\//, '')
        .replace(/\/$/, '');
      return `${local}@${domain}`;
    }

    // ========== Form submit → notify + Stripe Checkout ==========
    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      formError.classList.add('hidden');

      if (!selectedPlan) {
        showFormError('Please select a plan first.');
        document.getElementById('pricing').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        return;
      }

      if (!form.checkValidity()) {
        form.reportValidity();
        return;
      }

      const local = localPartInput.value.trim();
      if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/.test(local)) {
        showFormError('Email username can only contain letters, numbers, dots, hyphens and underscores.');
        return;
      }

      if (selectedPlan.id !== 'starter') {
        const domain = customDomainInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!/^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?(\.[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?)+$/.test(domain)) {
          showFormError('Please enter a valid domain (e.g. yourawesomewebsite.com).');
          return;
        }
      }

      formDataCache = {
        full_name: document.getElementById('full-name').value.trim(),
        contact_email: document.getElementById('contact-email').value.trim(),
        company: document.getElementById('company').value.trim(),
        phone: document.getElementById('phone').value.trim(),
        plan: selectedPlan.name,
        plan_id: selectedPlan.id,
        price: selectedPlan.price,
        desired_email: getDesiredEmail(),
        notes: document.getElementById('notes').value.trim()
      };

      submitBtn.disabled = true;
      submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Preparing checkout…';

      // 1. Notify you via email (FormSubmit)
      try {
        if (FORM_EMAIL && !FORM_EMAIL.includes('example.com')) {
          await fetch(`https://formsubmit.co/ajax/${FORM_EMAIL}`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              ...formDataCache,
              _subject: `New Business Email Signup — ${formDataCache.plan} ($${formDataCache.price}/mo)`,
              _template: 'table'
            })
          });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Form notification failed (continuing to Stripe):', err);
      }

      // 2. Go to Stripe Checkout
      try {
        await redirectToStripe();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        showFormError(err.message || 'Could not start payment. Please try again or contact support.');
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-lock text-sm"></i> Continue to Payment';
      }
    });

    function showFormError(msg) {
      formError.textContent = msg;
      formError.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    // ========== Stripe Checkout redirect ==========
    async function redirectToStripe() {
      // Prefer dynamic Checkout Session if endpoint is configured
      if (STRIPE.createSessionEndpoint) {
        showRedirectOverlay();
        const res = await fetch(STRIPE.createSessionEndpoint, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            plan: formDataCache.plan_id,
            price: formDataCache.price,
            customer_email: formDataCache.contact_email,
            customer_name: formDataCache.full_name,
            desired_email: formDataCache.desired_email,
            company: formDataCache.company,
            metadata: {
              desired_email: formDataCache.desired_email,
              plan: formDataCache.plan_id,
              source: 'registermysite-email-signup'
            }
          })
        });

        if (!res.ok) {
          const text = await res.text();
          throw new Error(`Checkout session failed (${res.status}): ${text}`);
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        if (!data.url) throw new Error('Server did not return a Checkout URL');
        window.location.href = data.url;
        return;
      }

      // Fallback: Payment Links (static / no-backend)
      const link = STRIPE.paymentLinks[formDataCache.plan_id];
      if (!link || link.includes('xxxx') || link.trim() === '') {
        hideRedirectOverlay();
        configWarning.classList.add('open');
        configWarning.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-lock text-sm"></i> Continue to Payment';
        return;
      }

      showRedirectOverlay();

      // Prefill email & pass reference data via query params
      const url = new URL(link);
      url.searchParams.set('prefilled_email', formDataCache.contact_email);
      // client_reference_id is useful for matching in webhooks / Stripe Dashboard
      url.searchParams.set('client_reference_id', formDataCache.desired_email);

      // Small delay so the user sees the overlay
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.location.href = url.toString();
      }, 600);
    }

    function showRedirectOverlay() {
      redirectModal.classList.add('open');
      redirectModal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function hideRedirectOverlay() {
      redirectModal.classList.remove('open');
      redirectModal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    document.getElementById('close-config-warning').addEventListener('click', () => {
      configWarning.classList.remove('open');
      configWarning.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    });

    // Escape key closes overlays
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (configWarning.classList.contains('open')) {
          configWarning.classList.remove('open');
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }
        if (successModal.classList.contains('open')) {
          successModal.classList.remove('open');
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }
      }
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html></doc><doc title="SEO Optimization" desc="Technical SEO, schema, and local SEO"><!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <!-- Google tag (gtag.js) -->
<script async src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-GMLQJKKPPP"></script>
<script>
  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
  gtag('js', new Date());

  gtag('config', 'G-GMLQJKKPPP');
</script>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <title>SEO Optimization — registermysite.com</title>
  <meta name="description" content="How RegisterMySite connects your business profiles to your website and applies modern SEO: structured data, FAQ schema, meta tags, sitemaps, and local search signals." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://registermysite.com/SEO_Optimization" />

  <!-- Example of what we implement for clients -->
  <script type="application/ld+json">
  {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "WebPage",
    "name": "SEO Optimization — registermysite.com",
    "description": "SEO services: link business accounts, FAQ schema, meta tags, sitemaps, and technical on-page optimization.",
    "url": "https://registermysite.com/SEO_Optimization",
    "isPartOf": {
      "@type": "WebSite",
      "name": "registermysite.com",
      "url": "https://registermysite.com"
    }
  }
  </script>

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css" />

  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');

    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
      background: #0a0a0f;
      color: #e2e8f0;
    }

    .section-title {
      font-family: 'Space Grotesk', sans-serif;
    }

    .gradient-text {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa, #f472b6);
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
    }

    .card {
      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    }

    .card:hover {
      border-color: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.22);
    }

    pre.code-block {
      background: #050508;
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 1.1rem 1.2rem;
      overflow-x: auto;
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      line-height: 1.55;
      color: #cbd5e1;
    }

    pre.code-block .key { color: #7dd3fc; }
    pre.code-block .str { color: #86efac; }
    pre.code-block .comment { color: #64748b; }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="min-h-screen">

  <!-- Header -->
  <header class="border-b border-white/10 sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl bg-black/60">
    <div class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between">
      <a href="/" class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-lg">R</div>
        <div>
          <span class="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">registermysite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
        </div>
      </a>
      <nav class="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-medium">
        <a href="/services" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Services</a>
        <a href="/Web_Design" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Web Design</a>
        <a href="/SEO_Optimization" class="text-blue-400">SEO</a>
        <a href="/contact" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Contact</a>
        <a href="/login" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Login</a>
      </nav>
    </div>
  </header>

  <!-- Hero -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pt-16 pb-12 text-center">
    <h1 class="section-title text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-5">
      SEO <span class="gradient-text">Optimization</span>
    </h1>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-lg max-w-2xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
      We connect your real-world business presence to your new website so search engines
      can understand who you are, where you operate, and what you offer — then we layer
      technical SEO that helps pages get discovered and understood.
    </p>
  </section>

  <!-- Linking accounts -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pb-16">
    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 relative overflow-hidden">
      <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600/10 via-transparent to-violet-600/5 pointer-events-none"></div>
      <div class="relative">
        <h2 class="section-title text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-4">
          Link your business accounts to your website
        </h2>
        <p class="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-3xl">
          Search engines don’t only look at a single page — they look for a consistent identity
          across the web. We help you point Google, social, and review profiles at your new domain
          so rankings and trust signals reinforce each other instead of competing.
        </p>

        <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-sm">
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-blue-400 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fab fa-google"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Google Business Profile</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Website URL, NAP (name, address, phone), categories, and posts aligned with your live site.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-red-400 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fab fa-yelp"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Yelp &amp; review sites</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Claimed listings with the same domain, contact info, and service areas as your pages.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-sky-400 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fab fa-facebook"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Facebook / Instagram</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Bio links, page “Website” fields, and consistent branding that match the new site.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-slate-200 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fab fa-x-twitter"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">X / LinkedIn / others</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Profile URLs and about sections updated so crawlers and customers land on the right domain.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-emerald-400 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fas fa-map-marker-alt"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Directories &amp; citations</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Consistent name/address/phone across major listings reduces confusion for local search.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-violet-400 text-lg mb-2"><i class="fas fa-share-nodes"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">On-site social proof</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Review carousels and profile links on the site close the loop between accounts and content.</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Technical SEO toolkit -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-3">
      Technical SEO we build <span class="gradient-text">into the site</span>
    </h2>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-center text-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-10">
      Small details compound. We implement the signals search engines actually parse —
      titles, descriptions, structured data, sitemaps, and clean HTML — not gimmicks.
    </p>

    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-tags text-blue-400"></i> Title tags &amp; meta descriptions
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
          Unique, readable <code class="text-slate-300">&lt;title&gt;</code> and meta description on each important page.
          They don’t “rank” by themselves, but they control how your result looks in Google and influence click-through.
        </p>
        <pre class="code-block">&lt;title&gt;Web Design in Los Angeles — Your Brand&lt;/title&gt;
&lt;meta <span class="key">name</span>=<span class="str">"description"</span> <span class="key">content</span>=<span class="str">"Custom sites you own. Clear process, fast delivery."</span>&gt;</pre>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-key text-amber-400"></i> Meta keywords <span class="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">(legacy)</span>
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
          We can include a focused keywords meta tag for completeness and older tooling.
          <strong class="text-slate-300">Google has not used meta keywords for ranking for many years</strong> —
          modern SEO prioritizes content, structure, and links instead. We never stuff irrelevant terms.
        </p>
        <pre class="code-block">&lt;meta <span class="key">name</span>=<span class="str">"keywords"</span> <span class="key">content</span>=<span class="str">"web design, LA, domain registration"</span>&gt;
<span class="comment">&lt;!-- Supportive only; not a ranking lever at Google --&gt;</span></pre>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6 md:col-span-2">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-code text-violet-400"></i> FAQ schema (JSON-LD structured data)
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
          FAQ blocks on your site can be marked up with <strong class="text-slate-200">FAQPage schema</strong>
          so search engines understand Q&amp;A content. That can enable rich results (expandable FAQs)
          when Google chooses to show them — and it helps machines map your services to real questions people ask.
        </p>
        <pre class="code-block">{
  <span class="key">"@context"</span>: <span class="str">"https://schema.org"</span>,
  <span class="key">"@type"</span>: <span class="str">"FAQPage"</span>,
  <span class="key">"mainEntity"</span>: [{
    <span class="key">"@type"</span>: <span class="str">"Question"</span>,
    <span class="key">"name"</span>: <span class="str">"How do I connect my domain to my website?"</span>,
    <span class="key">"acceptedAnswer"</span>: {
      <span class="key">"@type"</span>: <span class="str">"Answer"</span>,
      <span class="key">"text"</span>: <span class="str">"Update DNS nameservers or A/CNAME records at your registrar…"</span>
    }
  }]
}</pre>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-building text-emerald-400"></i> LocalBusiness / Organization schema
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Structured data for your business name, address, phone, service area (e.g. LA &amp; OC),
          and official social profile URLs. This ties the website entity to the same identity
          as your Google Business Profile and other listings.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-share-alt text-pink-400"></i> Open Graph &amp; Twitter Cards
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          When someone shares your page on Facebook, LinkedIn, iMessage, or X, the preview
          uses OG/Twitter meta tags — title, description, and image — so links look intentional, not broken.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-sitemap text-sky-400"></i> XML sitemap &amp; robots.txt
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          A clean <code class="text-slate-300">sitemap.xml</code> lists indexable URLs with priorities.
          <code class="text-slate-300">robots.txt</code> points crawlers at the sitemap and avoids blocking important paths.
          We submit the sitemap to Search Console when you have access.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-link text-orange-400"></i> Canonical URLs
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          <code class="text-slate-300">rel="canonical"</code> tells search engines which URL is the preferred version
          of a page — reducing duplicate-content confusion from tracking params, www vs non-www, or mirrored paths.
        </p>
      </article>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- More tricks -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-8">
      Other SEO details we care about
    </h2>
    <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-sm">
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-heading text-blue-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Heading hierarchy (H1–H3)</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">One clear H1 per page; logical subheadings that match how people search and scan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-image text-violet-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Image alt text &amp; file names</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">Descriptive alts for accessibility and context; compressed images for Core Web Vitals.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-mobile-alt text-pink-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Mobile-first layout</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">Responsive design so Google’s mobile indexing sees a usable, complete page.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-gauge-high text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Performance basics</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">Lean assets, sensible caching at the edge, and fast first paint — ranking systems notice slow sites.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-lock text-amber-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">HTTPS everywhere</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">SSL is table stakes; mixed content and broken certificates undermine trust and SEO.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-location-dot text-red-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Local landing clarity</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">Service-area language (e.g. LA / OC), contact NAP on the site matching your profiles.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-internal-link text-sky-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Internal linking</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">Services, tools, docs, and contact pages linked naturally so authority flows across the site.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 flex gap-3">
        <i class="fas fa-chart-line text-green-400 mt-1"></i>
        <div>
          <strong class="text-white">Search Console &amp; analytics handoff</strong>
          <p class="text-slate-400 mt-1">We help you verify the property, submit the sitemap, and keep ownership of tracking under your accounts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Honest note -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pb-16">
    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-8">
      <h2 class="section-title text-xl font-bold mb-3">What SEO is — and isn’t</h2>
      <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
        No ethical provider can guarantee #1 rankings. Algorithms change, competitors publish, and local
        pack results depend on reviews, proximity, and relevance. What we <em>can</em> do is remove technical
        obstacles, align your business profiles with your domain, mark up content correctly, and build
        pages that answer real search intent.
      </p>
      <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
        Because we code in-house and you own the site, SEO improvements live in <strong class="text-slate-200">your</strong>
        HTML and config — not inside a rented theme you can’t export.
      </p>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- CTA -->
  <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pb-20">
    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-10 text-center">
      <h2 class="section-title text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-3">
        Ready to connect your presence to a site that ranks cleaner?
      </h2>
      <p class="text-slate-400 max-w-xl mx-auto mb-8">
        From Google Business Profile to FAQ schema and sitemaps — we’ll wire the signals
        and leave you with code and accounts you control.
      </p>
      <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-4">
        <a href="/contact"
           class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-violet-600 hover:from-blue-400 hover:to-violet-500 text-white font-semibold px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl transition">
          <i class="fas fa-envelope"></i>
          Talk SEO with us
        </a>
        <a href="/Web_Design"
           class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-white/15 hover:border-white/30 font-semibold px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl transition">
          Web Design process
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Footer -->
  <footer class="border-t border-white/10 py-10">
    <div class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm text-slate-500">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-sm">R</div>
        <span>registermysite.com</span>
      </div>
      <p>© 2026 registermysite.com</p>
      <p class="italic">You dream. We code.</p>
    </div>
  </footer>

</body>
</html></doc><doc title="Web Design" desc="Responsive, conversion-focused websites"><!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <!-- Google tag (gtag.js) -->

  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <title>Web Design &amp; Development — RegisterMySite.com</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Custom websites built in-house. You own 100% of the code and data. Plus our AI HTML Editor chatbot at ai.editor.registermysite.com — the more detail you give the prompt, the better the page it builds." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://registermysite.com/Web_Design" />
  <link rel="icon" href="/assets/icons/favicon.ico" sizes="any">

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css" />

  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');

    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
      background: #0a0a0f;
      color: #e2e8f0;
    }

    .section-title {
      font-family: 'Space Grotesk', sans-serif;
    }

    .gradient-text {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa, #f472b6);
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
      background-clip: text;
    }

    .card {
      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
      transition: border-color 0.2s, background 0.2s;
    }

    .card:hover {
      border-color: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.28);
      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.045);
    }

    .cta-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #8b5cf6);
      color: #fff;
      transition: background 0.25s ease, transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.25s ease;
    }

    .cta-primary:hover {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #22d3ee, #a78bfa);
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 12px 32px rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.35);
    }

    .cta-secondary {
      background: transparent;
      color: #e2e8f0;
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
      transition: background 0.25s ease, border-color 0.25s ease, color 0.25s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
    }

    .cta-secondary:hover {
      background: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.15);
      border-color: #60a5fa;
      color: #93c5fd;
      transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    .nav-link {
      transition: color 0.15s ease;
    }
    .nav-link:hover {
      color: #60a5fa;
    }

    pre.code-block {
      background: #050508;
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 1.15rem 1.25rem;
      overflow-x: auto;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      line-height: 1.55;
      color: #cbd5e1;
    }

    pre.code-block .comment { color: #64748b; }
    pre.code-block .cmd { color: #7dd3fc; }
    pre.code-block .flag { color: #c4b5fd; }
    pre.code-block .str { color: #86efac; }

    .step-num {
      width: 2rem;
      height: 2rem;
      border-radius: 9999px;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-weight: 700;
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #8b5cf6);
      color: white;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }

    .logo-circle {
      display: inline-flex;
      width: 36px;
      height: 36px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden;
      margin-right: 6px;
      vertical-align: middle;
      border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
    }
    .logo-circle img {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      object-fit: cover;
    }

    /* Mobile menu */
    #mobile-menu { display: none; }
    #mobile-menu.open { display: block; }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="min-h-screen">

  <!-- ========== NAVIGATION (matches homepage) ========== -->
  <header class="border-b border-white/10 sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl bg-black/70">
    <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between h-16">
        <!-- Logo -->
        <a href="/" class="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <img src="https://uploads.registermysite.com/registermysite.gif" alt="RegisterMySite logo" class="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl object-cover bg-white/20">
          <div>
            <span class="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">RegisterMySite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
          </div>
        </a>

        <!-- Desktop nav -->
        <nav class="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-7 text-sm font-medium">
          <a href="/services" class="nav-link text-slate-300">Services</a>
          <a href="/pricing" class="nav-link text-slate-300">Pricing</a>
          <div class="relative group">
            <button type="button" class="nav-link text-slate-300 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              Tools <i class="fas fa-chevron-down text-[10px] opacity-70"></i>
            </button>
            <div class="invisible opacity-0 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-200 absolute top-full left-0 pt-2 w-56 z-50">
              <div class="card rounded-xl py-2 shadow-xl shadow-black/40">
                <a href="/tools/Domain_Search" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">Domain Search</a>
                <a href="/tools/TLDsearch" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">TLD Search</a>
                <a href="/tools/QR_Code" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">QR Code</a>
                <a href="/tools/og-preview" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">OG Preview</a>
                <a href="https://meta-gen.registermysite.com" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">Meta Generator</a>
                <a href="https://email-gen.registermysite.com" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">Email Template Generator</a>
                <a href="https://ai.editor.registermysite.com" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-blue-400">HTML Website Generator</a>
                <a href="/tools" class="block px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-blue-400 hover:bg-white/5">View all tools →</a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <a href="/Web_Design" class="nav-link text-blue-400">Web Design</a>
          <a href="/SEO_Optimization" class="nav-link text-slate-300">SEO</a>
          <a href="/docs" class="nav-link text-slate-300">Docs</a>
          <a href="/contact" class="nav-link text-slate-300">Contact</a>
          <a href="/login" class="cta-primary font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm">Login</a>
        </nav>

        <!-- Mobile menu button -->
        <button id="menu-toggle" type="button" class="lg:hidden text-slate-300 p-2" aria-label="Open menu">
          <i class="fas fa-bars text-xl"></i>
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Mobile nav -->
      <div id="mobile-menu" class="lg:hidden border-t border-white/10">
        <nav class="py-3 flex flex-col text-sm font-medium">
          <a href="/services" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">Services</a>
          <a href="/pricing" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">Pricing</a>
          <a href="/tools" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">Tools</a>
          <a href="/Web_Design" class="px-2 py-3 text-blue-400">Web Design</a>
          <a href="/SEO_Optimization" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">SEO</a>
          <a href="/docs" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">Docs</a>
          <a href="/contact" class="px-2 py-3 text-slate-300 hover:text-blue-400">Contact</a>
          <a href="/login" class="mx-2 mt-2 mb-3 cta-primary text-center font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl">Login / Sign up</a>
        </nav>
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <!-- Hero -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-16 pb-12 text-center">
    <p class="text-sm font-medium text-blue-400 tracking-wide uppercase mb-3">Web Development Agency</p>
    <h1 class="section-title text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-5">
      Web Design &amp; <span class="gradient-text">Development</span>
    </h1>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-lg max-w-2xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
      Custom sites built entirely in-house. You own the code. You own the data.
      No black-box page builders. No forced third-party lock-in.
    </p>
  </section>

  <!-- Ownership banner -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-14">
    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 relative overflow-hidden">
      <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600/10 via-transparent to-blue-600/5 pointer-events-none"></div>
      <div class="relative">
        <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 rounded-full px-3 py-1 mb-4">
          <i class="fas fa-key"></i>
          Full ownership
        </div>
        <h2 class="section-title text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-4">
          You completely own the code — and your data
        </h2>
        <p class="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-4 max-w-3xl">
          Every line we write for your project is delivered to <strong class="text-white">you</strong>.
          Source files, assets, configuration, deployment scripts — the whole repository.
          When the project is done, you are not renting a theme inside someone else’s platform.
          You hold the keys.
        </p>
        <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mt-6 text-sm">
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-4">
            <div class="text-emerald-400 mb-2"><i class="fas fa-code"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Code ownership</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Full source transfer. No proprietary runtime you can’t leave.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-4">
            <div class="text-blue-400 mb-2"><i class="fas fa-database"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Data ownership</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Your content, forms, and customer data stay under your control.</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-4">
            <div class="text-violet-400 mb-2"><i class="fas fa-house-laptop"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Built in-house</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Coded by our team — not outsourced to unknown freelancers or template farms.</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- AI HTML Editor -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-16">
    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 relative overflow-hidden">
      <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600/10 via-transparent to-pink-600/5 pointer-events-none"></div>
      <div class="relative">
        <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-300 bg-violet-500/10 border border-violet-500/20 rounded-full px-3 py-1 mb-4">
          <i class="fas fa-robot"></i>
          AI-powered tool
        </div>
        <h2 class="section-title text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-4">
          AI HTML Editor — <span class="gradient-text">ai.editor.registermysite.com</span>
        </h2>
        <p class="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-5 max-w-3xl">
          Need a quick HTML page or want to prototype before a full custom build?
          Our <strong class="text-white">HTML Editor with a built-in chatbot</strong> lets you describe the page you want in plain English.
          The AI agent generates clean, editable HTML right in the browser so you can iterate, copy the code, or hand it off for production.
        </p>

        <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 mb-6">
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-violet-400 mb-2 text-lg"><i class="fas fa-comments"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">Chatbot + live HTML editor</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed">
              Side-by-side chat and code panel. Tell the agent what you need — layout, sections, colors, forms, navigation — and it writes the HTML for you. Edit the output directly or keep prompting for changes.
            </span>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/30 border border-white/10 p-5">
            <div class="text-pink-400 mb-2 text-lg"><i class="fas fa-bullseye"></i></div>
            <strong class="text-white block mb-1">The more detail you give, the better the result</strong>
            <span class="text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed">
              Vague prompts produce generic pages. Specific prompts produce stronger output: page purpose, audience, sections you want, brand colors, tone, mobile behavior, CTAs, and any must-have elements. Richer context = cleaner structure and closer match to what you imagined.
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-3xl">
          <i class="fas fa-info-circle text-blue-400 mr-1.5"></i>
          This tool uses a <strong class="text-slate-300">similar UI</strong> (chat + editor layout) but a <strong class="text-slate-300">different AI agent</strong> than our
          <a href="https://email-gen.registermysite.com" class="text-blue-400 hover:underline">HTML Email Template Generator</a>.
          One is tuned for full web pages; the other is tuned for email-safe HTML templates.
        </p>

        <a href="https://ai.editor.registermysite.com"
           class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cta-primary font-semibold px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl">
          <i class="fas fa-external-link-alt"></i>
          Open AI HTML Editor
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Frameworks -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-3">
      Frameworks we <span class="gradient-text">apply</span>
    </h2>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-center text-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-10">
      We choose the stack that fits your goals — speed, SEO, interactivity, and how you want to maintain the site later.
      Everything remains portable and yours.
    </p>

    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fab fa-html5 text-orange-400"></i> Semantic HTML + Modern CSS
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Fast, accessible static and lightly interactive sites. Ideal for marketing pages, portfolios,
          service businesses, and content that must load instantly. No heavy JavaScript runtime unless you need it.
          Easy for you (or any developer) to edit later.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-wind text-sky-400"></i> Tailwind CSS
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Utility-first styling for consistent design systems and rapid iteration. We use it for production
          builds with a proper toolchain (not only the CDN) so you get optimized CSS and full control of the design tokens.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fab fa-react text-cyan-400"></i> React / Next-style patterns
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          When the product needs rich client interactivity, dashboards, or component-driven UI, we build with
          modern React patterns. Components stay in your repo — not locked inside a closed page builder.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-bolt text-yellow-400"></i> Cloudflare Workers + Pages
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Edge-first deployment: static assets at the CDN edge, APIs on Workers, optional D1 / KV / Durable Objects
          for data and sessions. Low latency worldwide, and you retain the project configuration and code.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-server text-emerald-400"></i> Node / API backends (when needed)
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          For traditional servers or hybrid setups we can structure Express-style or other Node APIs.
          Auth, forms, and business logic live in code you can host anywhere — your VPS, your cloud account, or Workers.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-database text-pink-400"></i> D1, KV &amp; structured storage
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          When your site needs accounts, forms, or app data, we design schemas and migrations you control.
          No forced SaaS CMS where leaving means exporting half-broken content. Your data model travels with the project.
        </p>
      </article>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Design process -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-10">
      Design process
    </h2>
    <div class="space-y-5">
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex gap-4">
        <span class="step-num">1</span>
        <div>
          <h3 class="font-semibold mb-1">Discovery &amp; goals</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            We clarify who the site is for, what actions matter (leads, bookings, sales), brand direction,
            and technical constraints. Optional in-person sessions in the LA / OC area, or remote workshops.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex gap-4">
        <span class="step-num">2</span>
        <div>
          <h3 class="font-semibold mb-1">Information architecture &amp; wireframes</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            Page structure, navigation, and content hierarchy before visual polish — so the site is clear
            on mobile and desktop before pixels are locked in.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex gap-4">
        <span class="step-num">3</span>
        <div>
          <h3 class="font-semibold mb-1">Visual design system</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            Typography, color, spacing, components, and motion that match your brand. We build a reusable
            system so new pages stay consistent without redesigning from scratch every time.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex gap-4">
        <span class="step-num">4</span>
        <div>
          <h3 class="font-semibold mb-1">Build in your repository</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            Implementation happens in a project you own (or that is transferred to you). HTML/CSS/JS or
            component frameworks, assets, env examples, and deploy config — all versioned and documented.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex gap-4">
        <span class="step-num">5</span>
        <div>
          <h3 class="font-semibold mb-1">Review, harden, launch</h3>
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            Responsive QA, basic SEO foundations, performance checks, forms/auth smoke tests, then
            production deploy. You receive access, credentials handoff notes, and ownership of the stack.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Wrangler CLI deployment -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-3">
      Deployment with <span class="gradient-text">Wrangler CLI</span>
    </h2>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-center text-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-10">
      We deploy many projects on Cloudflare using the official Wrangler command-line tool.
      You can run the same commands from your machine — no mystery “publish” button inside a locked dashboard.
    </p>

    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 space-y-8">
      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold text-lg mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <i class="fas fa-terminal text-blue-400"></i> What Wrangler is
        </h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          <strong class="text-slate-200">Wrangler</strong> is Cloudflare’s CLI for Workers, Pages-style assets,
          D1 databases, KV namespaces, secrets, and Durable Objects. From a single project folder you can
          develop locally, apply database migrations, set secrets, and ship to production — all from the terminal.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">1. Project layout (typical)</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="comment"># Example structure we hand off</span>
my-site/
├── package.json
├── wrangler.toml          <span class="comment"># bindings: D1, KV, DO, assets</span>
├── schema.sql             <span class="comment"># D1 migrations</span>
├── public/                <span class="comment"># static HTML, CSS, images</span>
│   ├── index.html
│   └── ...
└── src/
    └── index.ts           <span class="comment"># Worker / API entry</span></pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">2. Install &amp; authenticate</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="cmd">npm</span> install
<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> login
<span class="comment"># Opens browser to link your Cloudflare account</span></pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">3. Create resources (one-time)</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> d1 create my-site-db
<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> kv namespace create OAUTH_STATE
<span class="comment"># Paste returned IDs into wrangler.toml</span>

<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> d1 execute my-site-db <span class="flag">--file</span>=./schema.sql
<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> d1 execute my-site-db <span class="flag">--local</span> <span class="flag">--file</span>=./schema.sql</pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">4. Secrets (never commit these)</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> secret put SESSION_SECRET
<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> secret put GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID
<span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> secret put GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET
<span class="comment"># Values are stored encrypted in your Cloudflare account</span></pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">5. Local development</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> dev
<span class="comment"># Serves the Worker + public/ assets locally</span>
<span class="comment"># Hot reload while you iterate on HTML and API routes</span></pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">6. Production deploy</h3>
        <pre class="code-block"><span class="cmd">npx wrangler</span> deploy
<span class="comment"># Ships Worker code, static assets, and binding config</span>
<span class="comment"># Your custom domain is attached in the Cloudflare dashboard</span></pre>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-3">7. After launch — still yours</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
          Because the project is standard files + <code class="text-slate-300">wrangler.toml</code>, you can:
        </p>
        <ul class="text-sm text-slate-400 space-y-2">
          <li class="flex gap-2"><i class="fas fa-check text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i> Clone the repo on any machine and run <code class="text-slate-300">wrangler dev</code> / <code class="text-slate-300">deploy</code></li>
          <li class="flex gap-2"><i class="fas fa-check text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i> Change DNS, domains, and Cloudflare settings under <em>your</em> account</li>
          <li class="flex gap-2"><i class="fas fa-check text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i> Hire another developer later without buying your site back from a closed platform</li>
          <li class="flex gap-2"><i class="fas fa-check text-emerald-400 mt-1"></i> Export or migrate data using scripts and schema you already own</li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Why in-house -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-16">
    <h2 class="section-title text-3xl font-bold text-center mb-8">
      Why “in-house” matters
    </h2>
    <div class="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-2">No third-party page-builder lock-in</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Drag-and-drop builders are convenient until you need a custom feature, a data export, or a move
          to another host. We ship real source so you’re never trapped paying rent for your own homepage.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-2">Accountability &amp; quality</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          The same team that designs also implements and deploys. Fewer handoffs, clearer standards,
          and documentation written for the people who will maintain the site — including you.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-2">Security &amp; privacy posture</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Fewer mystery plugins and unknown vendors in the critical path. Secrets live in your Cloudflare
          account; application logic lives in your repo; customer data follows a schema you can audit.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="card rounded-2xl p-6">
        <h3 class="font-semibold mb-2">Long-term cost control</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Edge hosting and static assets are efficient. You’re not stacked with per-page SaaS fees for
          basic content. When you grow, you extend the codebase instead of starting over on a new platform.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- CTA -->
  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pb-20">

                       placeholder="Enter domain name (e.g. example.com)"

                <p class="text-slate-300 text-sm leading-relaxed mb-3">
                    A <strong>Top-Level Domain (TLD)</strong> is the last part of a domain name — the letters after the final dot.
                    Examples include <strong>.com</strong>, <strong>.org</strong>, <strong>.net</strong>, and newer options like
                    <strong>.app</strong>, <strong>.dev</strong>, <strong>.shop</strong>, and <strong>.xyz</strong>.

            WHOIS data powered by public APIs • Direct registration available through registermysite.com • For assistance email <a href="/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection" class="__cf_email__" data-cfemail="e093818c8593a092858789939485928d9993899485ce838f8d">[email&#160;protected]</a>

    <script data-cfasync="false" src="/cdn-cgi/scripts/5c5dd728/cloudflare-static/email-decode.min.js"></script><script>
        document.getElementById('search-btn').addEventListener('click', performLookup);
        document.getElementById('domain-input').addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') performLookup();
        });

        async function performLookup() {
            let input = document.getElementById('domain-input').value.trim();
            if (!input) {
                alert('Please enter a domain name!');
                return;
            }

            input = input.toLowerCase();
            const resultDiv = document.getElementById('result');
            const statusTitle = document.getElementById('status-title');
            const statusMessage = document.getElementById('status-message');
            const registerLink = document.getElementById('register-link');
            const detailsDiv = document.getElementById('whois-details');

            resultDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
            statusTitle.textContent = 'Looking up...';
            statusTitle.className = 'text-2xl font-bold mb-2 text-blue-400';
            statusMessage.textContent = 'Fetching WHOIS data from public API...';
            registerLink.style.display = 'none';
            detailsDiv.textContent = '';

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`https://www.whatismyip.net/whois/?domain=${encodeURIComponent(input)}`);
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('API request failed');
                const data = await response.json();

                const isAvailable = data.ldhName === null || (data.events && data.events[0] && data.events[0].eventDate === null);

                if (isAvailable) {
                    statusTitle.textContent = 'Domain Available!';
                    statusTitle.className = 'text-2xl font-bold mb-2 available';
                    statusMessage.textContent = `Great news! ${input} is available for registration.`;
                    registerLink.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                    // Direct registration link on registermysite.com
                    registerLink.href = `https://registermysite.com/register?domain=${encodeURIComponent(input)}`;
                    registerLink.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Register ${input} Now`;
                } else {
                    statusTitle.textContent = 'Domain is Taken';
                    statusTitle.className = 'text-2xl font-bold mb-2 taken';
                    statusMessage.textContent = `${input} is already registered.`;
                }

                let details = '';
                if (data.entities) {
                    const registrarEntity = data.entities.find(e => e.roles && e.roles.includes('registrar'));
                    if (registrarEntity && registrarEntity.vcardArray && registrarEntity.vcardArray[1]) {
                        const registrarName = registrarEntity.vcardArray[1].find(item => item[0] === 'fn')?.[3];
                        if (registrarName && registrarName !== 'N/A') details += `Registrar: ${registrarName}\n`;
                    }
                }

                if (data.events) {
                    const registrationEvent = data.events.find(e => e.eventAction === 'registration');
                    if (registrationEvent && registrationEvent.eventDate) {
                        details += `Created: ${new Date(registrationEvent.eventDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n`;
                    }
                    const expirationEvent = data.events.find(e => e.eventAction === 'expiration');
                    if (expirationEvent && expirationEvent.eventDate) {
                        details += `Expires: ${new Date(expirationEvent.eventDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n`;
                    }
                    const lastChangedEvent = data.events.find(e => e.eventAction === 'last changed');
                    if (lastChangedEvent && lastChangedEvent.eventDate) {
                        details += `Updated: ${new Date(lastChangedEvent.eventDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n`;
                    }
                }

                detailsDiv.textContent = details || 'No additional details available.';
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                statusTitle.textContent = 'Limited Data Available';
                statusTitle.className = 'text-2xl font-bold mb-2 text-amber-400';
                statusMessage.innerHTML = `Could not retrieve full WHOIS details for ${input}.<br>You can still attempt registration.`;
                registerLink.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                // Direct registration link on registermysite.com
                registerLink.href = `https://registermysite.com/register?domain=${encodeURIComponent(input)}`;
                registerLink.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Register ${input} Now`;
                detailsDiv.textContent = 'Public API may be temporarily limited.';
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html></doc><doc title="HTML Email Generator" desc="AI-powered email-safe HTML templates"><!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
	<head>
		<meta charset="UTF-8" />
		<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
		<title>HTML Email Template Generator</title>
		<style>
			:root {
				--primary-color: #f6821f;
				--primary-hover: #e67e22;
				--light-bg: #f9fafb;
				--border-color: #e5e7eb;
				--text-color: #1f2937;
				--text-light: #6b7280;
				--user-msg-bg: #fff2e6;
				--assistant-msg-bg: #f3f4f6;
				--code-bg: #1e1e1e;
				--code-text: #d4d4d4;
			}

			* {
				box-sizing: border-box;
				margin: 0;
				padding: 0;
			}

			body {
				font-family:
					-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu,
					Cantarell, sans-serif;
				line-height: 1.6;
				color: var(--text-color);
				height: 100vh;
				display: flex;
				flex-direction: column;
				overflow: hidden;
			}

			header {
				text-align: center;
				padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
				border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background: white;
				flex-shrink: 0;
			}

			h1 {
				font-size: 1.35rem;
				color: var(--primary-color);
			}

			header p {
				font-size: 0.9rem;
				color: var(--text-light);
			}

			.app-container {
				display: flex;
				flex: 1;
				min-height: 0;
				gap: 0;
			}

			/* Chat panel */
			.chat-panel {
				flex: 0 0 380px;
				display: flex;
				flex-direction: column;
				border-right: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background: white;
				min-width: 300px;
			}

			.chat-messages {
				flex: 1;
				overflow-y: auto;
				padding: 1rem;
				background-color: var(--light-bg);
			}

			.message {
				margin-bottom: 0.85rem;
				padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem;
				border-radius: 8px;
				max-width: 95%;
			}

			.message p {
				white-space: pre-line;
				margin: 0;
				overflow-wrap: anywhere;
				font-size: 0.95rem;
			}

			.user-message {
				background-color: var(--user-msg-bg);
				margin-left: auto;
			}

			.assistant-message {
				background-color: var(--assistant-msg-bg);
			}

			.typing-indicator {
				display: none;
				font-style: italic;
				color: var(--text-light);
				padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
				font-size: 0.9rem;
			}

			.typing-indicator.visible {
				display: block;
			}

			.message-input {
				display: flex;
				padding: 0.75rem;
				border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background-color: white;
				gap: 0.5rem;
			}

			#user-input {
				flex: 1;
				padding: 0.7rem;
				border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				border-radius: 6px;
				font-family: inherit;
				resize: none;
				min-height: 44px;
				max-height: 120px;
				font-size: 0.95rem;
			}

			#send-button {
				padding: 0 1.1rem;
				background-color: var(--primary-color);
				color: white;
				border: none;
				border-radius: 6px;
				cursor: pointer;
				transition: background-color 0.2s;
				font-weight: 500;
			}

			#send-button:hover {
				background-color: var(--primary-hover);
			}

			#send-button:disabled {
				background-color: var(--text-light);
				cursor: not-allowed;
			}

			/* Editor panel */
			.editor-panel {
				flex: 1;
				display: flex;
				flex-direction: column;
				min-width: 0;
				background: #fafafa;
			}

			.editor-toolbar {
				display: flex;
				align-items: center;
				gap: 0.5rem;
				padding: 0.55rem 0.9rem;
				border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background: white;
				flex-shrink: 0;
				flex-wrap: wrap;
			}

			.editor-toolbar button {
				padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;
				border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				border-radius: 5px;
				background: white;
				cursor: pointer;
				font-size: 0.875rem;
				font-weight: 500;
				color: var(--text-color);
				transition: all 0.15s;
			}

			.editor-toolbar button:hover {
				background: var(--light-bg);
				border-color: #d1d5db;
			}

			.editor-toolbar button.primary {
				background: var(--primary-color);
				color: white;
				border-color: var(--primary-color);
			}

			.editor-toolbar button.primary:hover {
				background: var(--primary-hover);
			}

			.editor-toolbar button.export-btn {
				background: #059669;
				color: white;
				border-color: #059669;
			}

			.editor-toolbar button.export-btn:hover {
				background: #047857;
			}

			.editor-toolbar .spacer {
				flex: 1;
			}

			.editor-toolbar .label {
				font-size: 0.8rem;
				color: var(--text-light);
				margin-right: 0.25rem;
			}

			.editor-content {
				flex: 1;
				display: flex;
				min-height: 0;
				position: relative;
			}

			#html-code {
				flex: 1;
				width: 100%;
				height: 100%;
				font-family: "SF Mono", "Fira Code", "Consolas", "Monaco", monospace;
				font-size: 13px;
				line-height: 1.5;
				padding: 1rem;
				border: none;
				resize: none;
				background: var(--code-bg);
				color: var(--code-text);
				tab-size: 2;
				outline: none;
			}

			#preview-container {
				display: none;
				flex: 1;
				flex-direction: column;
				border-left: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background: white;
				min-width: 0;
				overflow: hidden;
			}

			#preview-container.visible {
				display: flex;
			}

			.preview-header {
				padding: 0.4rem 0.75rem;
				background: #f3f4f6;
				border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				font-size: 0.8rem;
				color: var(--text-light);
				flex-shrink: 0;
			}

			#preview-frame {
				flex: 1;
				width: 100%;
				height: 100%;
				border: none;
				background: white;
			}

			.editor-content.preview-open #html-code {
				flex: 1;
				max-width: 50%;
			}

			footer {
				padding: 0.4rem 1rem;
				text-align: center;
				font-size: 0.8rem;
				color: var(--text-light);
				border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				background: white;
				flex-shrink: 0;
			}

			@media (max-width: 800px) {
				.app-container {
					flex-direction: column;
				}
				.chat-panel {
					flex: 0 0 40%;
					border-right: none;
					border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				}
				.editor-content.preview-open #html-code {
					max-width: 100%;
				}
				#preview-container.visible {
					border-left: none;
					border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);
				}
			}
		</style>
	</head>
	<body>
		<header>
			<h1>HTML Email Template Generator</h1>
			<p>Describe an email → get email-safe HTML you can edit, preview, copy, download, or export to the composer</p>
		</header>

		<div class="app-container">
			<!-- Left: Chat -->
			<div class="chat-panel">
				<div id="chat-messages" class="chat-messages">
					<div class="message assistant-message">
						<p>
							Hi! Describe the email you want (e.g. “a clean product launch email with a hero image, two feature blocks, and a big CTA button”) and I’ll generate email-safe HTML using tables + inline CSS. You can edit it live, preview it, then export it to the email composer.
						</p>
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="typing-indicator" id="typing-indicator">
					AI is generating email HTML...
				</div>

				<div class="message-input">
					<textarea
						id="user-input"
						placeholder="Describe the email template you want..."
						rows="1"
						autofocus
					></textarea>
					<button id="send-button">Generate</button>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- Right: HTML Editor + Preview -->
			<div class="editor-panel">
				<div class="editor-toolbar">
					<span class="label">Email HTML</span>
					<button id="toggle-preview" class="primary">Show Preview</button>
					<button id="copy-html" title="Copy HTML to clipboard">Copy</button>
					<button id="download-html" title="Download as .html file">Download</button>
					<button id="export-email" class="export-btn" title="Open email composer with this HTML">
						Export to Composer
					</button>
					<div class="spacer"></div>
					<span class="label" id="status-label">Ready</span>
				</div>
				<div class="editor-content" id="editor-content">
					<textarea
						id="html-code"
						spellcheck="false"
						placeholder="Generated email-safe HTML will appear here..."
					></textarea>
					<div id="preview-container">
						<div class="preview-header">Live Preview (scrollable) — note: real email clients may render slightly differently</div>
						<iframe id="preview-frame" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin" title="Email Preview"></iframe>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<footer>
			<p>HTML Email Template Generator • Built for email-safe (tables + inline CSS) output</p>
		</footer>

		<script src="chat.js"></script>
	</body>
</html></doc><doc title="AI HTML Editor" desc="Describe a site and get editable HTML"><!doctype html>

			#preview-frame {

				width: 100%;
				height: 100%;
				border: none;
				background: white;
				/* Allows the rendered page to scroll inside the iframe */

			/* When preview is visible, code takes half */
			.editor-content.preview-open #html-code {
				flex: 1;
				max-width: 50%;

			footer {

	<body>
		<header>

			<p>Describe any website — the AI writes complete HTML you can edit live and preview</p>
		</header>

		<div class="app-container">
			<!-- Left: Chat -->
			<div class="chat-panel">
				<div id="chat-messages" class="chat-messages">
