<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <title>AI Meta Title &amp; Description Generator — registermysite.com</title>
  <meta name="description" content="AI-powered SEO meta title and description generator using Cloudflare Workers AI. Instant suggestions with SERP preview." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://registermysite.com/tools/meta-gen" />

  <meta property="og:title" content="AI Meta Title & Description Generator — registermysite.com" />
  <meta property="og:description" content="Generate SEO titles and descriptions with Cloudflare Workers AI." />
  <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
  <meta property="og:url" content="https://registermysite.com/tools/meta-gen" />

  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css" />

  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');

    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
      background: #0a0a0f;
      color: #e2e8f0;
    }

    .section-title {
      font-family: 'Space Grotesk', sans-serif;
    }

    .gradient-text {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa, #f472b6);
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
    }

    .card {
      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    }

    label.field-label {
      display: block;
      margin-bottom: 0.4rem;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: #94a3b8;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    }

    input[type="text"],
    textarea {
      width: 100%;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
      color: #e2e8f0;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 12px 14px;
      font-size: 0.95rem;
      transition: border-color 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
    }

    input[type="text"]:focus,
    textarea:focus {
      outline: none;
      border-color: #60a5fa;
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.15);
    }

    textarea {
      min-height: 140px;
      resize: vertical;
    }

    .btn-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #8b5cf6);
    }

    .btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa);
    }

    .btn-primary:disabled {
      opacity: 0.65;
      cursor: wait;
    }

    .suggestion {
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
      padding: 12px 12px 48px 12px;
      border-radius: 12px;
      margin: 10px 0;
      position: relative;
      min-height: 88px;
      transition: border-color 0.2s, background 0.2s;
    }

    .suggestion:hover {
      border-color: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.3);
      background: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.06);
    }

    .suggestion strong {
      display: block;
      margin-bottom: 6px;
      line-height: 1.4;
      font-weight: 500;
      color: #e2e8f0;
    }

    .stats {
      font-size: 12px;
      color: #64748b;
    }

    .warning {
      color: #f87171;
    }

    .copy-btn {
      position: absolute;
      bottom: 10px;
      right: 10px;
      background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2);
      color: #6ee7b7;
      border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
      padding: 6px 10px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 13px;
      line-height: 1;
      transition: all 0.15s ease;
      z-index: 3;
    }

    .copy-btn:hover {
      background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .serp-preview {
      background: #fff;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 16px 18px;
      font-family: Arial, sans-serif;
    }

    .serp-title {
      color: #1a0dab;
      font-size: 18px;
      margin: 0 0 4px;
      line-height: 1.3;
    }

    .serp-url {
      color: #006621;
      font-size: 14px;
    }

    .serp-desc {
      color: #545454;
      font-size: 14px;
      margin-top: 4px;
      line-height: 1.45;
    }

    .toast {
      position: fixed;
      bottom: 24px;
      left: 50%;
      transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(80px);
      background: #064e3b;
      color: #a7f3d0;
      border: 1px solid #34d399;
      padding: 10px 18px;
      border-radius: 10px;
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: all 0.25s ease;
      z-index: 100;
      pointer-events: none;
    }

    .toast.show {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
    }

    .source-badge {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.35rem;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      padding: 0.25rem 0.6rem;
      border-radius: 9999px;
    }

    .source-ai {
      background: rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.15);
      color: #c4b5fd;
      border: 1px solid rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.3);
    }

    .source-fallback {
      background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12);
      color: #fcd34d;
      border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="min-h-screen">

  <header class="border-b border-white/10 sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl bg-black/60">
    <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between">
      <a href="/" class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-lg">R</div>
        <div>
          <span class="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">registermysite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
        </div>
      </a>
      <nav class="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-medium">
        <a href="/tools" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">All Tools</a>
        <a href="/SEO_Optimization" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">SEO</a>
        <a href="/docs" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Docs</a>
        <a href="/login" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Login</a>
      </nav>
    </div>
  </header>

  <section class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 pt-12 pb-8 text-center">
    <h1 class="section-title text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold mb-3">
      Meta Title &amp; <span class="gradient-text">Description</span> Generator
    </h1>
    <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm md:text-base max-w-xl mx-auto">
      Paste your topic or page content → get SEO title and description suggestions
      powered by <strong class="text-slate-300">Cloudflare Workers AI</strong>.
    </p>
  </section>

  <main class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 pb-16">

    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 mb-8">
      <div class="mb-5">
        <label class="field-label" for="content">Page content / topic / keywords</label>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-2">Include main keyword, benefits, audience, and what the page offers.</p>
        <textarea id="content" placeholder="Example: Custom web design in Los Angeles. Domains, SEO, business email. You own the code — registermysite.com."></textarea>
      </div>
      <div class="mb-5">
        <label class="field-label" for="brand">Brand / site name</label>
        <input type="text" id="brand" value="RegisterMySite" placeholder="Your brand" />
      </div>
      <button type="button" id="generate-btn" onclick="generateSuggestions()"
              class="btn-primary w-full text-white font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        <i class="fas fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i>
        <span id="generate-label">Generate with AI</span>
      </button>
      <p id="status-line" class="text-xs text-slate-500 text-center mt-3 min-h-[1rem]"></p>
    </div>

    <div id="results" class="hidden">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
        <span id="source-badge" class="source-badge source-ai"><i class="fas fa-brain"></i> Workers AI</span>
      </div>
      <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
        <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6">
          <h2 class="section-title font-bold text-lg mb-1">Meta titles</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-3">Aim: 50–60 characters (~580 px)</p>
          <div id="titles"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6">
          <h2 class="section-title font-bold text-lg mb-1">Meta descriptions</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-3">Aim: 140–160 characters</p>
          <div id="descriptions"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6">
          <h2 class="section-title font-bold text-lg mb-3">SERP preview</h2>
          <div class="serp-preview" id="serpPreview">
            <div class="serp-title">Selected title</div>
            <div class="serp-url">https://registermysite.com/example</div>
            <div class="serp-desc">Selected description appears here…</div>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-4 leading-relaxed">
            Real Google results vary by device and query. Pixel width is an estimate.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card rounded-2xl p-5 mt-8 text-sm text-slate-400">
      <strong class="text-white"><i class="fas fa-info-circle text-blue-400 mr-2"></i>How it works</strong>
      <p class="mt-2 leading-relaxed">
        Requests go to your Cloudflare LLM chat Worker at <code class="text-slate-300">/api/chat</code>
        (same API as the chat template). If AI is unavailable, a local template fallback still runs.
        Always edit suggestions for accuracy and brand voice before publishing.
      </p>
    </div>
  </main>

  <footer class="border-t border-white/10 py-10">
    <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm text-slate-500">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-sm">R</div>
        <span>registermysite.com</span>
      </div>
      <p>Powered by Cloudflare Workers AI · <a href="/tools" class="text-blue-400 hover:underline">More tools</a></p>
      <p class="italic">You dream. We code.</p>
    </div>
  </footer>

  <div id="toast" class="toast">Copied to clipboard</div>

  <script>
    /**
     * Point this at your deployed LLM chat Worker if meta-gen is on a different host.
     * Same origin (recommended): leave as "" so fetch goes to /api/chat on registermysite.com
     * Separate worker: "https://your-llm-chat.workers.dev"
     */
    const API_BASE = "";

    const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are an expert SEO copywriter. Given page content and a brand name, produce meta titles and meta descriptions.

Rules:
- Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences, no commentary.
- JSON shape:
{
  "titles": ["title1", "title2", "title3"],
  "descriptions": ["desc1", "desc2", "desc3"]
}
- Exactly 3 titles and 3 descriptions.
- Titles: ideally 50-60 characters, include primary keyword and brand when natural.
- Descriptions: ideally 140-160 characters, compelling, include a benefit and soft CTA.
- No keyword stuffing. Sound human and trustworthy.`;

    async function generateSuggestions() {
      const input = document.getElementById('content').value.trim();
      const brand = document.getElementById('brand').value.trim() || 'YourSite';
      const btn = document.getElementById('generate-btn');
      const label = document.getElementById('generate-label');
      const status = document.getElementById('status-line');

      if (!input) {
        showToast('Please enter some content or keywords');
        return;
      }

      btn.disabled = true;
      label.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Generating…';
      status.textContent = 'Calling Cloudflare Workers AI…';

      let payload = null;
      let source = 'ai';

      try {
        const text = await callChatAPI(input, brand);
        payload = parseMetaJSON(text);
        if (!payload) throw new Error('Could not parse AI response as JSON');
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('AI generate failed, using fallback:', err);
        payload = localFallback(input, brand);
        source = 'fallback';
        status.textContent = 'AI unavailable — used local fallback templates.';
      }

      renderResults(payload, source);
      if (source === 'ai') status.textContent = 'Generated with Workers AI.';

      btn.disabled = false;
      label.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i> Generate with AI';
    }

    /** POST /api/chat — same contract as Cloudflare llm-chat-app-template chat.js */
    async function callChatAPI(content, brand) {
      const messages = [
        { role: 'system', content: SYSTEM_PROMPT },
        {
          role: 'user',
          content: `Brand: ${brand}\n\nPage content / topic:\n${content}`,
        },
      ];

      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/chat`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ messages }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`API ${response.status}`);
      }
      if (!response.body) {
        throw new Error('Empty response body');
      }

      // Stream SSE / chunked Workers AI output (same as chat.js)
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
      let responseText = '';
      let buffer = '';

      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) {
          const parsed = consumeSseEvents(buffer + '\n\n');
          for (const data of parsed.events) {
            if (data === '[DONE]') break;
            responseText += extractChunk(data);
          }
          break;
        }

        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const parsed = consumeSseEvents(buffer);
        buffer = parsed.rest;
        for (const data of parsed.events) {
          if (data === '[DONE]') continue;
          responseText += extractChunk(data);
        }
      }

      // Non-stream JSON fallback
      if (!responseText.trim()) {
        try {
          const cloneText = await response.clone().text();
          const j = JSON.parse(cloneText);
          if (typeof j.response === 'string') responseText = j.response;
        } catch (_) { /* ignore */ }
      }

      return responseText.trim();
    }

    function extractChunk(data) {
      try {
        const jsonData = JSON.parse(data);
        if (typeof jsonData.response === 'string') return jsonData.response;
        if (jsonData.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content) return jsonData.choices[0].delta.content;
        if (jsonData.choices?.[0]?.message?.content) return jsonData.choices[0].message.content;
      } catch (_) {
        // plain text chunk
        if (data && data !== '[DONE]') return data;
      }
      return '';
    }

    function consumeSseEvents(chunk) {
      const events = [];
      let rest = chunk;
      let idx;
      while ((idx = rest.indexOf('\n\n')) !== -1) {
        const raw = rest.slice(0, idx);
        rest = rest.slice(idx + 2);
        const lines = raw.split('\n');
        for (const line of lines) {
          if (line.startsWith('data:')) {
            events.push(line.slice(5).trim());
          }
        }
      }
      // Also handle newline-delimited JSON without SSE prefix
      if (events.length === 0 && chunk.includes('{')) {
        const lines = chunk.split('\n').filter(Boolean);
        for (const line of lines) {
          const t = line.replace(/^data:\s*/, '').trim();
          if (t) events.push(t);
        }
        rest = '';
      }
      return { events, rest };
    }

    function parseMetaJSON(text) {
      if (!text) return null;
      // Strip markdown fences if model adds them
      let cleaned = text.replace(/```json\s*/gi, '').replace(/```\s*/g, '').trim();
      // Find outermost JSON object
      const start = cleaned.indexOf('{');
      const end = cleaned.lastIndexOf('}');
      if (start === -1 || end === -1) return null;
      cleaned = cleaned.slice(start, end + 1);
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(cleaned);
        if (!Array.isArray(data.titles) || !Array.isArray(data.descriptions)) return null;
        return {
          titles: data.titles.slice(0, 3).map(String),
          descriptions: data.descriptions.slice(0, 3).map(String),
        };
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }

    function localFallback(input, brand) {
      const words = input.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length > 3);
      const mainKeyword = words[0] || 'website';
      const secondary = words.slice(1, 4).join(' ') || 'online tools';
      const benefitWords = ['free', 'fast', 'easy', 'best', '2026', 'instant', 'powerful'];

      const titleTemplates = [
        `{keyword} | {benefit} {secondary} – {brand}`,
        `Best {keyword} in 2026 – {benefit} & {secondary} | {brand}`,
        `{benefit} {keyword} Online – Free, Fast | {brand}`,
      ];
      const descTemplates = [
        `Discover the best {keyword} on {brand}. {benefit}, no signup required. {secondary} in seconds – perfect for 2026.`,
        `Create {keyword} instantly with {brand}. Enjoy {benefit} features and {secondary} today!`,
        `Looking for {benefit} {keyword}? {brand} offers fast, privacy-first {secondary}. Start now!`,
      ];

      const titles = titleTemplates.map((t, i) => {
        let title = t
          .replace('{keyword}', capitalize(mainKeyword))
          .replace('{secondary}', capitalize(secondary))
          .replace('{benefit}', benefitWords[i % benefitWords.length])
          .replace('{brand}', brand)
          .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
          .trim();
        if (title.length > 70) title = title.substring(0, 67) + '...';
        return title;
      });

      const descriptions = descTemplates.map((t, i) => {
        let desc = t
          .replace('{keyword}', mainKeyword)
          .replace('{secondary}', secondary)
          .replace('{benefit}', benefitWords[(i + 2) % benefitWords.length])
          .replace('{brand}', brand)
          .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
          .trim();
        if (desc.length > 170) desc = desc.substring(0, 167) + '...';
        return desc;
      });

      return { titles, descriptions };
    }

    function renderResults(payload, source) {
      const titlesDiv = document.getElementById('titles');
      const descsDiv = document.getElementById('descriptions');
      const badge = document.getElementById('source-badge');

      titlesDiv.innerHTML = '';
      descsDiv.innerHTML = '';

      if (source === 'ai') {
        badge.className = 'source-badge source-ai';
        badge.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-brain"></i> Workers AI';
      } else {
        badge.className = 'source-badge source-fallback';
        badge.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-bolt"></i> Local fallback';
      }

      payload.titles.forEach((title) => {
        const charCount = title.length;
        const approxPx = estimatePixelWidth(title);
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'suggestion';
        el.innerHTML = `
          <strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong>
          <div class="stats">Chars: ${charCount} · ~${approxPx}px ${approxPx > 600 ? '<span class="warning">(may truncate)</span>' : ''}</div>
          <button type="button" class="copy-btn" data-copy="${escapeAttr(title)}" title="Copy">
            <i class="fas fa-copy"></i> Copy
          </button>
        `;
        titlesDiv.appendChild(el);
      });

      payload.descriptions.forEach((desc) => {
        const charCount = desc.length;
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'suggestion';
        el.innerHTML = `
          <strong>${escapeHtml(desc)}</strong>
          <div class="stats">Chars: ${charCount} ${charCount > 160 ? '<span class="warning">(may truncate on desktop)</span>' : ''}</div>
          <button type="button" class="copy-btn" data-copy="${escapeAttr(desc)}" title="Copy">
            <i class="fas fa-copy"></i> Copy
          </button>
        `;
        descsDiv.appendChild(el);
      });

      document.getElementById('serpPreview').innerHTML = `
        <div class="serp-title">${escapeHtml(payload.titles[0] || '')}</div>
        <div class="serp-url">https://registermysite.com/example</div>
        <div class="serp-desc">${escapeHtml(payload.descriptions[0] || '')}</div>
      `;

      document.getElementById('results').classList.remove('hidden');
      bindCopyButtons();
    }

    function bindCopyButtons() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.copy-btn').forEach((btn) => {
        btn.onclick = () => {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(btn.getAttribute('data-copy') || '').then(() => {
            showToast('Copied to clipboard');
          }).catch(() => showToast('Copy failed'));
        };
      });
    }

    function showToast(msg) {
      const t = document.getElementById('toast');
      t.textContent = msg;
      t.classList.add('show');
      setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2000);
    }

    function estimatePixelWidth(str) {
      let px = str.length * 9.5;
      for (const char of str) {
        if ('mwMW'.includes(char)) px += 3;
        if ('Iil'.includes(char)) px -= 2;
      }
      return Math.round(px);
    }

    function capitalize(str) {
      return str.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + str.slice(1);
    }

    function escapeHtml(text) {
      return String(text)
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
    }

    function escapeAttr(text) {
      return escapeHtml(text).replace(/\n/g, ' ');
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html></doc></tools><optional><doc title="Login"><!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <title>Login — registermysite.com</title>
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css" />
  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');
    body { font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e2e8f0; }
    .section-title { font-family: 'Space Grotesk', sans-serif; }
    .gradient-text {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa, #f472b6);
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
    }
    .card { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="min-h-screen">

  <header class="border-b border-white/10 sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl bg-black/60">
    <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between">
      <a href="/" class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-lg">R</div>
        <div>
          <span class="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">registermysite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
        </div>
      </a>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main class="max-w-md mx-auto px-6 pt-16 pb-20">
    <div class="text-center mb-10">
      <h1 class="section-title text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold mb-3">
        Welcome <span class="gradient-text">Back</span>
      </h1>
      <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm">Sign in to manage domains and collaborate on your website.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="card rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 space-y-4">
      <div id="error" class="hidden rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300"></div>

      <!-- Google -->
      <a href="/api/auth/google"
         class="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 w-full bg-white hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-900 font-semibold py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl transition">
        <svg class="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path fill="#4285F4" d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"/>
          <path fill="#34A853" d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"/>
          <path fill="#FBBC05" d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z"/>
          <path fill="#EA4335" d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z"/>
        </svg>
        Continue with Google
      </a>

      <!-- GitHub -->
      <a href="/api/auth/github"
         class="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 w-full bg-[#24292f] hover:bg-[#32383f] text-white font-semibold py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl transition">
        <i class="fab fa-github text-xl"></i>
        Continue with GitHub
      </a>

      <p class="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 pt-4">
        By continuing you agree to our Terms of Service and Privacy Policy.
      </p>
    </div>
  </main>

  <script>
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const error = params.get('error');
    if (error) {
      const el = document.getElementById('error');
      const messages = {
        access_denied: 'You cancelled the sign-in.',
        invalid_state: 'Security check failed. Please try again.',
        email_not_verified: 'Your email is not verified.',
        oauth_failed: 'Sign-in failed. Please try again.',
        missing_params: 'Invalid response from provider.',
      };
      el.textContent = messages[error] || `Error: ${error}`;
      el.classList.remove('hidden');
    }
  </script>
</body>

<html lang="en">
<head>

    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Register Domain — registermysite.com</title>
    <meta name="description" content="Complete your domain registration. Fast, secure, and at wholesale pricing.">

    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css">

    <style>
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');

        body {
            font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
            background: #0a0a0f;
            color: #e2e8f0;
        }

        .section-title {
            font-family: 'Space Grotesk', sans-serif;
        }

        .gradient-text {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #60a5fa, #a78bfa, #f472b6);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .card {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
        }

        input, select, textarea {
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.45);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
            color: #e2e8f0;
            border-radius: 12px;
            padding: 13px 16px;
            width: 100%;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }

        input:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus {
            outline: none;
            border-color: #60a5fa;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(96,165,250,0.15);
        }

        label {
            display: block;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            color: #94a3b8;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        }

        .required::after {
            content: " *";
            color: #f87171;
        }

        /* Payment Modal */
        #payment-modal {
            position: fixed;
            inset: 0;
            z-index: 100;
            display: none;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            padding: 16px;
        }

        #payment-modal.open {
            display: flex;
        }

        #payment-overlay {
            position: absolute;
            inset: 0;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.75);
            backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        }

        #payment-content {
            position: relative;
            background: #111118;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
            border-radius: 20px;
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 480px;
            max-height: 90vh;
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
            animation: modalIn 0.25s ease;
        }

        @keyframes modalIn {
            from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.95) translateY(10px); }
            to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1) translateY(0); }
        }

        #payment-iframe {
            width: 100%;
            height: 520px;
            border: none;
            background: #0a0a0f;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body class="min-h-screen">

    <!-- Header -->
    <header class="border-b border-white/10 sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl bg-black/60">
        <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-lg">R</div>
                <div>
                    <span class="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">registermysite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
                </div>
            </div>
            <nav class="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-medium">
                <a href="/tools.html" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">Tools</a>
                <a href="/tools/whois-lookup.html" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition">WHOIS Lookup</a>
            </nav>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- Hero -->
    <section class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 pt-12 pb-8 text-center">
        <p class="text-blue-400 font-semibold tracking-widest uppercase text-sm mb-3">Domain Registration</p>
        <h1 class="section-title text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold mb-3">
            Complete Your <span class="gradient-text">Registration</span>
        </h1>
        <p class="text-slate-400 max-w-xl mx-auto">
            You’re one step away from owning your domain. Fill in the details below to finish registration.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- Domain Banner -->
    <section class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-6 pb-8">
        <div id="domain-banner" class="card rounded-2xl p-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-blue-500/25 bg-blue-500/5">
            <div>
                <p class="text-xs text-blue-400 font-medium uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Domain being registered</p>
                <p id="display-domain" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold font-mono text-white">—</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full">
                    <i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-1"></i> Available
                </span>
            </div>
        </div>
        <p id="no-domain-msg" class="hidden text-center text-amber-400 text-sm mt-4">
            No domain specified. Please start from a domain search or include <code>?domain=yourdomain.com</code> in the URL.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- Registration Form -->
    <section class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-6 pb-20">
        <form id="registration-form" class="card rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 space-y-8" onsubmit="handleSubmit(event)">

            <!-- Domain -->
            <div>
                <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-5 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-globe text-blue-400"></i> Domain Details
                </h2>
                <div class="grid gap-5">
                    <div>
                        <label for="domain" class="required">Domain Name</label>
                        <input type="text" id="domain" name="domain" required
                               placeholder="example.com"
                               class="font-mono text-lg">
                    </div>
                    <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                        <div>
                            <label for="years">Registration Period</label>
                            <select id="years" name="years">
                                <option value="1">1 Year</option>
                                <option value="2">2 Years</option>
                                <option value="3" selected>3 Years</option>
                                <option value="5">5 Years</option>
                                <option value="10">10 Years</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label for="privacy">WHOIS Privacy</label>
                            <select id="privacy" name="privacy">
                                <option value="yes" selected>Enabled (Recommended)</option>
                                <option value="no">Disabled</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Registrant Contact -->
            <div>
                <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-5 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-user text-violet-400"></i> Registrant Contact
                </h2>
                <div class="grid gap-5">
                    <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                        <div>
                            <label for="first-name" class="required">First Name</label>
                            <input type="text" id="first-name" name="first_name" required>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label for="last-name" class="required">Last Name</label>
                            <input type="text" id="last-name" name="last_name" required>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="email" class="required">Email Address</label>
                        <input type="email" id="email" name="email" required placeholder="you@example.com">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="phone" class="required">Phone Number</label>
                        <input type="tel" id="phone" name="phone" required placeholder="+1 (555) 123-4567">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="organization">Organization / Company (optional)</label>
                        <input type="text" id="organization" name="organization" placeholder="Your Business LLC">
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Address -->
            <div>
                <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-5 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-map-marker-alt text-emerald-400"></i> Address
                </h2>
                <div class="grid gap-5">
                    <div>
                        <label for="address1" class="required">Street Address</label>
                        <input type="text" id="address1" name="address1" required>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="address2">Address Line 2 (optional)</label>
                        <input type="text" id="address2" name="address2">
                    </div>
                    <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
                        <div>
                            <label for="city" class="required">City</label>
                            <input type="text" id="city" name="city" required>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label for="state" class="required">State / Province</label>
                            <input type="text" id="state" name="state" required>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label for="zip" class="required">Postal Code</label>
                            <input type="text" id="zip" name="zip" required>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="country" class="required">Country</label>
                        <select id="country" name="country" required>
                            <option value="US" selected>United States</option>
                            <option value="CA">Canada</option>
                            <option value="GB">United Kingdom</option>
                            <option value="AU">Australia</option>
                            <option value="DE">Germany</option>
                            <option value="FR">France</option>
                            <option value="NL">Netherlands</option>
                            <option value="OTHER">Other</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Nameservers -->
            <div>
                <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-5 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-server text-amber-400"></i> Nameservers <span class="text-slate-500 text-sm font-normal">(optional)</span>
                </h2>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-4">Leave blank to use our default nameservers. You can change them later.</p>
                <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                    <div>
                        <label for="ns1">Nameserver 1</label>
                        <input type="text" id="ns1" name="ns1" placeholder="ns1.registermysite.com">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="ns2">Nameserver 2</label>
                        <input type="text" id="ns2" name="ns2" placeholder="ns2.registermysite.com">
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Terms -->
            <div class="pt-2">
                <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer normal-case tracking-normal text-sm text-slate-300">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="terms" name="terms" required
                           class="mt-1 w-4 h-4 accent-blue-500 rounded">
                    <span>
                        I agree to the <a href="/terms" class="text-blue-400 hover:underline">Terms of Service</a>
                        and <a href="/privacy" class="text-blue-400 hover:underline">Privacy Policy</a>.
                        I confirm that the contact information provided is accurate.
                    </span>
                </label>
            </div>

            <!-- Submit -->
            <div class="pt-4">
                <button type="submit" id="submit-btn"
                        class="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-violet-600 hover:from-blue-500 hover:to-violet-500 text-white font-semibold text-lg py-4 rounded-2xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
                    <i class="fas fa-lock"></i>
                    <span id="submit-text">Complete Registration</span>
                </button>
                <p id="form-error" class="text-red-400 text-sm text-center mt-3 hidden"></p>
                <p class="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 mt-4">
                    Secure registration • At-cost wholesale pricing • Instant activation
                </p>
            </div>
        </form>
    </section>

    <!-- Payment Modal -->
    <div id="payment-modal">
        <div id="payment-overlay" onclick="closePaymentModal()"></div>
        <div id="payment-content">
            <!-- Modal Header -->
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/10">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Complete Payment</p>
                    <p id="modal-domain" class="font-mono font-semibold text-blue-300"></p>
                </div>
                <button onclick="closePaymentModal()"
                        class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center transition">
                    <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
                </button>
            </div>

            <!-- Iframe: CashApp Pay -->
            <iframe id="payment-iframe"
                    src="https://registermysite.com/tools/CashAppPay"
                    title="Cash App Pay"
                    allow="payment"></iframe>

            <div class="px-5 py-3 border-t border-white/10 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                Secure payment powered by Cash App • Close this window after payment is complete
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Footer -->
    <footer class="border-t border-white/10 py-12">
        <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 text-sm text-slate-500">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-sm">R</div>
                <span>registermysite.com</span>
            </div>
            <p>© 2026 registermysite.com — Domain Registration</p>
            <p class="italic">You dream. We code.</p>
        </div>
    </footer>

    <script>
        // ============================================================
        // ⚠️  REPLACE THIS with your real Cloudflare Worker URL
        // ============================================================
        const WORKER_URL = 'https://registermysitecheckout.americanmadediecast.workers.dev';
        // ============================================================

        function getDomainFromURL() {
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            let domain = params.get('domain') || '';
            domain = domain.trim().toLowerCase()
                .replace(/^https?:\/\//, '')
                .replace(/^www\./, '')
                .split('/')[0];
            return domain;
        }

        function init() {
            const domain = getDomainFromURL();
            const domainInput = document.getElementById('domain');
            const displayDomain = document.getElementById('display-domain');
            const noDomainMsg = document.getElementById('no-domain-msg');
            const banner = document.getElementById('domain-banner');

            if (domain) {
                domainInput.value = domain;
                displayDomain.textContent = domain;
                document.title = `Register ${domain} — registermysite.com`;
            } else {
                displayDomain.textContent = 'No domain specified';
                noDomainMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
                banner.classList.remove('border-blue-500/25', 'bg-blue-500/5');
                banner.classList.add('border-amber-500/25', 'bg-amber-500/5');
            }

            domainInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                displayDomain.textContent = domainInput.value.trim() || '—';
            });
        }

        async function handleSubmit(e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            const form = document.getElementById('registration-form');
            const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
            const submitText = document.getElementById('submit-text');
            const formError = document.getElementById('form-error');

            const domain = document.getElementById('domain').value.trim();
            if (!domain) {
                formError.textContent = 'Please enter a domain name.';
                formError.classList.remove('hidden');
                return;
            }

            // Collect all form data
            const payload = {
                domain: domain,
                years: document.getElementById('years').value,
                privacy: document.getElementById('privacy').value,
                first_name: document.getElementById('first-name').value.trim(),
                last_name: document.getElementById('last-name').value.trim(),
                email: document.getElementById('email').value.trim(),
                phone: document.getElementById('phone').value.trim(),
                organization: document.getElementById('organization').value.trim(),
                address1: document.getElementById('address1').value.trim(),
                address2: document.getElementById('address2').value.trim(),
                city: document.getElementById('city').value.trim(),
                state: document.getElementById('state').value.trim(),
                zip: document.getElementById('zip').value.trim(),
                country: document.getElementById('country').value,
                ns1: document.getElementById('ns1').value.trim(),
                ns2: document.getElementById('ns2').value.trim(),
                submitted_at: new Date().toISOString(),
                source: window.location.href
            };

            // UI loading state
            submitBtn.disabled = true;
            submitText.textContent = 'Submitting...';
            formError.classList.add('hidden');

            try {
                const response = await fetch(WORKER_URL, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });

                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`Server responded with ${response.status}`);
                }

                // Optional: read response if your worker returns data
                // const result = await response.json();

                // Success → open payment modal
                openPaymentModal(domain);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Registration POST failed:', err);
                formError.textContent = 'Unable to submit registration. Please try again or contact support.';
                formError.classList.remove('hidden');
            } finally {
                submitBtn.disabled = false;
                submitText.textContent = 'Complete Registration';
            }
        }

        function openPaymentModal(domain) {
            document.getElementById('modal-domain').textContent = domain;
            document.getElementById('payment-modal').classList.add('open');
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closePaymentModal() {
            document.getElementById('payment-modal').classList.remove('open');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }

        // Close modal with Escape key
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') closePaymentModal();
        });

        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html></doc><doc title="Promo"><!DOCTYPE html>

  <style>
    :root {
      --cash-green: #00D632;
      --cash-dark: #00C853;
      --bg: #0a0a0a;
      --card: #141414;
      --text: #ffffff;
      --muted: #a0a0a0;
      --border: #2a2a2a;
    }

    * {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      box-sizing: border-box;
    }

    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
      background: var(--bg);
      color: var(--text);
      line-height: 1.6;
      min-height: 100vh;
    }

    .container {
      max-width: 720px;
      margin: 0 auto;
      padding: 0 20px;
    }

    /* Header */
    header {
      padding: 24px 0;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
      text-align: center;
    }

    .logo {
      font-size: 1.25rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      letter-spacing: -0.02em;
      color: var(--text);
      text-decoration: none;
    }

    .logo span {
      color: var(--cash-green);
    }

    /* Hero */
    .hero {
      padding: 60px 0 40px;
      text-align: center;
    }

    .badge {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
      background: rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.12);
      color: var(--cash-green);
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      padding: 8px 16px;
      border-radius: 100px;
      margin-bottom: 24px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.25);
    }

    .badge::before {
      content: '';
      width: 8px;
      height: 8px;
      background: var(--cash-green);
      border-radius: 50%;
      animation: pulse 1.5s infinite;
    }

    @keyframes pulse {
      0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
      50% { opacity: 0.4; }
    }

    h1 {
      font-size: clamp(2rem, 5vw, 2.75rem);
      font-weight: 800;
      letter-spacing: -0.03em;
      line-height: 1.15;
      margin-bottom: 16px;
    }

    h1 .highlight {
      color: var(--cash-green);
    }

    .subtitle {
      font-size: 1.125rem;
      color: var(--muted);
      max-width: 520px;
      margin: 0 auto 32px;
    }

    /* CTA Button */
    .cta {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      gap: 10px;
      background: var(--cash-green);
      color: #000;
      font-size: 1.125rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      padding: 18px 36px;
      border-radius: 12px;
      text-decoration: none;
      transition: transform 0.15s ease, box-shadow 0.15s ease;
      box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.35);
      border: none;
      cursor: pointer;
    }

    .cta:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 8px 28px rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.45);
      background: var(--cash-dark);
    }

    .cta svg {
      width: 22px;
      height: 22px;
    }

    .terms-note {
      margin-top: 16px;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      color: var(--muted);
    }

    /* Steps */
    .steps {
      padding: 20px 0 40px;
    }

    .steps-title {
      text-align: center;
      font-size: 1.25rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      margin-bottom: 32px;
    }

    .step-list {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 16px;
    }

    .step {
      display: flex;
      gap: 20px;
      background: var(--card);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 16px;
      padding: 24px;
      align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .step-num {
      flex-shrink: 0;
      width: 40px;
      height: 40px;
      background: rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.15);
      color: var(--cash-green);
      font-weight: 700;
      font-size: 1.125rem;
      border-radius: 10px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
    }

    .step-content h3 {
      font-size: 1.05rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      margin-bottom: 4px;
    }

    .step-content p {
      font-size: 0.95rem;
      color: var(--muted);
    }

    .code-box {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border: 1px dashed var(--cash-green);
      padding: 6px 14px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      font-family: 'SF Mono', 'Fira Code', monospace;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--cash-green);
      margin-top: 8px;
      letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    }

    /* Domain Search Section */
    .domain-search {
      padding: 20px 0 50px;
    }

    .search-card {
      background: var(--card);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 20px;
      padding: 32px 24px;
      text-align: center;
    }

    .search-card h2 {
      font-size: 1.4rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      margin-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .search-card > p {
      color: var(--muted);
      margin-bottom: 24px;
      font-size: 0.95rem;
    }

    .search-form {
      display: flex;
      gap: 10px;
      max-width: 480px;
      margin: 0 auto;
    }

    .search-input {
      flex: 1;
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 14px 18px;
      font-size: 1rem;
      color: var(--text);
      font-family: inherit;
      outline: none;
      transition: border-color 0.2s;
    }

    .search-input:focus {
      border-color: var(--cash-green);
    }

    .search-input::placeholder {
      color: #666;
    }

    .search-btn {
      background: var(--cash-green);
      color: #000;
      font-weight: 700;
      font-size: 1rem;
      padding: 14px 24px;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 12px;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background 0.15s, transform 0.15s;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }

    .search-btn:hover {
      background: var(--cash-dark);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
    }

    .search-btn:disabled {
      opacity: 0.6;
      cursor: not-allowed;
      transform: none;
    }

    .search-hint {
      margin-top: 14px;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      color: #666;
    }

    /* Results */
    #results {
      margin-top: 28px;
      display: none;
      text-align: left;
    }

    #results.visible {
      display: block;
    }

    .results-header {
      font-size: 0.95rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      margin-bottom: 14px;
      color: var(--muted);
      text-align: center;
    }

    .result-grid {
      display: grid;
      gap: 12px;
    }

    .result-item {
      background: #1a1a1a;
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 16px 18px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 12px;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .result-item.available {
      border-color: rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.45);
      background: rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.07);
    }

    .result-item.taken {
      border-color: rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.3);
    }

    .result-item.unknown {
      border-color: rgba(255, 193, 7, 0.3);
    }

    .result-domain {
      font-weight: 600;
      font-size: 1.05rem;
      word-break: break-all;
    }

    .result-status {
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      padding: 4px 12px;
      border-radius: 100px;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    }

    .result-status.available {
      background: rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.2);
      color: var(--cash-green);
    }

    .result-status.taken {
      background: rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.15);
      color: #ff6b6b;
    }

    .result-status.unknown {
      background: rgba(255, 193, 7, 0.15);
      color: #ffc107;
    }

    .result-actions {
      display: flex;
      gap: 8px;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .result-link {
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      color: var(--cash-green);
      text-decoration: underline;
    }

    .loading {
      text-align: center;
      padding: 20px;
      color: var(--muted);
    }

    .spinner {
      display: inline-block;
      width: 20px;
      height: 20px;
      border: 2px solid rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.3);
      border-top-color: var(--cash-green);
      border-radius: 50%;
      animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
      margin-right: 10px;
      vertical-align: middle;
    }

    @keyframes spin {
      to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
    }

    .disclaimer-note {
      margin-top: 16px;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      color: #666;
      text-align: center;
      line-height: 1.4;
    }

    /* Offer Card */
    .offer {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.08), rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.02));
      border: 1px solid rgba(0, 214, 50, 0.25);
      border-radius: 20px;
      padding: 32px 24px;
      text-align: center;
      margin-bottom: 48px;
    }

    .offer h2 {
      font-size: 1.5rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      margin-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .offer p {
      color: var(--muted);
      margin-bottom: 24px;
    }

    .price-row {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: center;
      align-items: baseline;
      gap: 12px;
      margin-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .old-price {
      font-size: 1.25rem;
      color: var(--muted);
      text-decoration: line-through;
    }

    .new-price {
      font-size: 2.5rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      color: var(--cash-green);
    }

    /* Footer */
    footer {
      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
      padding: 32px 0;
      text-align: center;
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      color: var(--muted);
    }

    footer a {
      color: var(--muted);
      text-decoration: underline;
    }

    footer a:hover {
      color: var(--text);
    }

    .disclaimer {
      max-width: 560px;
      margin: 16px auto 0;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      line-height: 1.5;
      color: #666;
    }

    @media (max-width: 480px) {
      .hero {
        padding: 40px 0 30px;
      }
      .cta {
        width: 100%;
        padding: 16px 24px;
      }
      .step {
        padding: 20px;
      }
      .search-form {
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      .search-btn {
        width: 100%;
      }
      .result-item {
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: flex-start;
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header>
     <!-- <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between h-16">
         Logo
        <a href="/" class="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <img src="https://uploads.registermysite.com/registermysite.gif" alt="Logo" class="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl object-cover bg-white/20">
        <div>
         <span class="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">RegisterMySite</span><span class="text-blue-400">.com</span>
          </div>
        </a> -->
  </header>

  <main>
    <section class="hero">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="badge">Limited Time Offer</div>
        <h1>Get a <span class="highlight">FREE Domain Name</span><br>for Your Website</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">
          Sign up for Cash App with referral code <strong>53B7SPT</strong>, send $5 or more, get $5 back — and claim your free domain name registration on RegisterMySite.
        </p>
        <a href="https://cash.app/refer/53B7SPT" class="cta" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M12 2C6.48 2 2 6.48 2 12s4.48 10 10 10 10-4.48 10-10S17.52 2 12 2zm-1 14H9V8h2v8zm4 0h-2V8h2v8z"/></svg>
          Claim Free Domain via Cash App
        </a>
        <p class="terms-note">Terms apply · Must complete Cash App steps to qualify</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="steps">
      <div class="container">
        <h2 class="steps-title">How to Get Your Free Domain Name</h2>
        <div class="step-list">
          <div class="step">
            <div class="step-num">1</div>
            <div class="step-content">
              <h3>Create a Cash App account</h3>
              <p>Download Cash App and sign up using this exclusive referral code:</p>
              <div class="code-box">53B7SPT</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="step">
            <div class="step-num">2</div>
            <div class="step-content">
              <h3>Send $5 or more</h3>
              <p>Link a debit card and send at least $5 to anyone within 14 days. You’ll receive $5 back from Cash App.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="step">
            <div class="step-num">3</div>
            <div class="step-content">
              <h3>Claim your free domain</h3>
              <p>After completing the Cash App steps, return here or contact us with proof to receive your free domain registration on RegisterMySite.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="step">
            <div class="step-num">4</div>
            <div class="step-content">
              <h3>Search for your domain</h3>
              <p>Use the live domain availability checker below to find an available name for your free registration.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Domain Search Functionality - Client-side -->
    <section class="domain-search" id="domain-search">
      <div class="container">
        <div class="search-card">
          <h2>Live Domain Availability Check</h2>
          <p>Enter a name (with or without extension). We’ll check popular TLDs instantly using DNS lookup.</p>

          <form class="search-form" id="domainSearchForm">
            <input
              type="text"
              id="domainInput"
              class="search-input"
              placeholder="e.g. myawesomebrand or myawesomebrand.com"
              required
              autocomplete="off"
              spellcheck="false"
            >
            <button type="submit" class="search-btn" id="searchBtn">Check Availability</button>
          </form>

          <p class="search-hint">Checks .com .net .org .io .dev .app and more · Client-side DNS lookup</p>

          <div id="results">
            <div class="results-header" id="resultsHeader"></div>
            <div class="result-grid" id="resultGrid"></div>
            <p class="disclaimer-note">
              Results are based on DNS resolution (Google DNS). Domains that do not resolve are likely available, but final availability and pricing are confirmed at registration. Always verify on <a href="https://registermysite.com/tools/Domain_Search" style="color: var(--cash-green);" target="_blank" rel="noopener">RegisterMySite Domain Search</a>.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section>
      <div class="container">
        <div class="offer">
          <h2>Your Free Domain Name Awaits</h2>
          <p>Standard domain registration starts at regular price. This limited-time Cash App promo covers a free 1-year domain name registration for eligible users who complete the referral.</p>
          <div class="price-row">
            <span class="old-price">$12+</span>
            <span class="new-price">FREE</span>
          </div>
          <a href="https://cash.app/refer/53B7SPT" class="cta" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="margin-top: 20px;">
            Start Now — Get $5 + Free Domain
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>

  <footer>
    <div class="container">
      <p>© 2026 RegisterMySite · <a href="https://registermysite.com">registermysite.com</a></p>
      <p class="disclaimer">
        This is an independent promotional offer. Cash App is a registered trademark of Block, Inc.
        Referral bonus is provided by Cash App subject to their terms. Free domain offer is provided by RegisterMySite
        and requires successful completion of the Cash App referral process. One free domain per qualifying user.
        Domain TLD and availability subject to change. Additional terms may apply. Not affiliated with Cash App / Block, Inc.
      </p>
    </div>
  </footer>

  <script>
    const POPULAR_TLDS = ['com', 'net', 'org', 'io', 'dev', 'app', 'co', 'xyz', 'me', 'info'];

    const form = document.getElementById('domainSearchForm');
    const input = document.getElementById('domainInput');
    const btn = document.getElementById('searchBtn');
    const resultsEl = document.getElementById('results');
    const resultsHeader = document.getElementById('resultsHeader');
    const resultGrid = document.getElementById('resultGrid');

    function cleanDomainInput(raw) {
      let v = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
      v = v.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').replace(/^www\./, '');
      // remove trailing slash or path
      v = v.split('/')[0].split('?')[0];
      return v;
    }

    function parseNameAndTld(value) {
      // If user already included a TLD
      const parts = value.split('.');
      if (parts.length >= 2) {
        const tld = parts.pop();
        const name = parts.join('.');
        return { name, tld, hasTld: true };
      }
      return { name: value, tld: null, hasTld: false };
    }

    async function checkViaGoogleDNS(domain) {
      try {
        const url = `https://dns.google/resolve?name=${encodeURIComponent(domain)}&type=A`;
        const res = await fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('DNS request failed');
        const data = await res.json();

        // Status codes: 0 = NOERROR, 3 = NXDOMAIN
        if (data.Status === 3) {
          return 'available'; // NXDOMAIN → almost certainly available
        }
        if (data.Status === 0 && (data.Answer || data.Authority)) {
          return 'taken';
        }
        // Fallback: try NS
        const nsUrl = `https://dns.google/resolve?name=${encodeURIComponent(domain)}&type=NS`;
        const nsRes = await fetch(nsUrl);
        const nsData = await nsRes.json();
        if (nsData.Status === 0 && (nsData.Answer || nsData.Authority)) {
          return 'taken';
        }
        if (nsData.Status === 3) {
          return 'available';
        }
        return 'unknown';
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('DNS check failed for', domain, err);
        return 'unknown';
      }
    }

    function createResultItem(domain, status) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = `result-item ${status}`;

      const statusLabel = status === 'available' ? 'Available' : status === 'taken' ? 'Taken' : 'Check Manually';

      div.innerHTML = `
        <div class="result-domain">${domain}</div>
        <div class="result-status ${status}">${statusLabel}</div>
        <div class="result-actions">
          ${status === 'available'
            ? `<a class="result-link" href="https://registermysite.com/tools/Domain_Search?q=${encodeURIComponent(domain)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Register Free →</a>`
            : `<a class="result-link" href="https://registermysite.com/tools/Domain_Search?q=${encodeURIComponent(domain)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">View details</a>`
          }
        </div>
      `;
      return div;
    }

    form.addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();

      const raw = input.value;
      const cleaned = cleanDomainInput(raw);
      if (!cleaned || cleaned.length < 2) {
        alert('Please enter a valid domain name (at least 2 characters).');
        input.focus();
        return;
      }

      const { name, tld, hasTld } = parseNameAndTld(cleaned);

      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Checking…';
      resultsEl.classList.add('visible');
      resultGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><span class="spinner"></span>Checking DNS records…</div>';
      resultsHeader.textContent = '';

      const domainsToCheck = [];
      if (hasTld) {
        domainsToCheck.push(cleaned);
      } else {
        POPULAR_TLDS.forEach(t => domainsToCheck.push(`${name}.${t}`));
      }

      const checks = domainsToCheck.map(async (d) => {
        const status = await checkViaGoogleDNS(d);
        return { domain: d, status };
      });

      const results = await Promise.all(checks);

      // Sort: available first, then unknown, then taken
      results.sort((a, b) => {
        const order = { available: 0, unknown: 1, taken: 2 };
        return order[a.status] - order[b.status];
      });

      resultGrid.innerHTML = '';
      results.forEach(r => {
        resultGrid.appendChild(createResultItem(r.domain, r.status));
      });

      const availableCount = results.filter(r => r.status === 'available').length;
      resultsHeader.textContent = availableCount > 0
        ? `${availableCount} likely available · ${results.length} checked`
        : `No clear available matches · ${results.length} checked`;

      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.textContent = 'Check Availability';
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html></doc></optional></project>
